+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {

+        }
+    }

+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
 
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    if (const char* overrideValue = std::getenv("SPMV_CUDA_THREADS_PER_ROW")) {
+        const unsigned value = static_cast<unsigned>(std::atoi(overrideValue));
+        if (value == 1 || value == 2 || value == 4 || value == 8 ||
+            value == 16 || value == 32) {
+            return value;
+        }
+    }
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+    if (average <= 2.0) return 1;
+    if (average <= 4.0) return 2;
+    if (average <= 8.0) return 4;
+    if (average <= 16.0) return 8;
+    if (average <= 32.0) return 16;
+    return 32;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+    }
+}
+

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
 
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    if (const char* overrideValue = std::getenv("SPMV_CUDA_THREADS_PER_ROW")) {
+        const unsigned value = static_cast<unsigned>(std::atoi(overrideValue));
+        if (value == 1 || value == 2 || value == 4 || value == 8 ||
+            value == 16 || value == 32) {
+            return value;
+        }
+    }
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+    if (average <= 2.0) return 1;
+    if (average <= 4.0) return 2;
+    if (average <= 8.0) return 4;
+    if (average <= 16.0) return 8;
+    if (average <= 32.0) return 16;
+    return 32;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out); break;
+    }
+}
+

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}

+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}

Correctness is holding across every subgroup specialization and matrix edge case tested. The tuned occupancy rule plus CUDA Graph batching materially improves repeated-launch scalability: on the RTX 3090, a 4096×4096 case now sustains about 90 GFLOP/s, and an 8192×8192 case about 118 GFLOP/s, both with validation passing. I’m doing the final sanitizer, clean-build, and semantic edge checks now.

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = (numRows + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
 
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK);
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}
+

+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
 
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK);
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}
+

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
+// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
+    if (!source.empty()) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
+// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
+// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
+// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
+// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                   const index_t numRows,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
+                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    if (row < numRows) {
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
 
+    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK);
+    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
+}
+
+unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
+    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
+
+    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
+    unsigned width = 32;
+    if (average <= 1.0) {
+        width = 1;
+    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
+        width = 4;
+    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
+        width = 8;
+    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
+        width = 16;
+    }
+
+    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
+    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
+    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
+                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
+    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
+        width *= 2;
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    switch (threadsPerRow) {
+        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
+    }
+}
+
+cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
+                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
+                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
+                                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launchCount == 0) {
+        return nullptr;
+    }
+
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
+        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    return executable;
+}

    19	// Fail immediately on CUDA errors: this benchmark is intentionally GPU-only
    20	// and must never silently fall back to a serial implementation.
    21	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
    22	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
    23	        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
    24	                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    25	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    26	    }
    27	}

    31	template <typename T>
    32	class DeviceBuffer {
    33	  public:
    34	    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
    35	        if (count_ != 0) {
    36	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
    37	        }
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    ~DeviceBuffer() {
    41	        if (data_ != nullptr) {
    42	            cudaFree(data_);
    43	        }
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    47	    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    48	
    49	    T* get() { return data_; }
    50	    const T* get() const { return data_; }
    51	    size_t bytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
    52	
    53	  private:
    54	    T* data_ = nullptr;
    55	    size_t count_ = 0;
    56	};
    57	
    58	template <typename T>
    59	void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination, const std::vector<T>& source) {
    60	    if (!source.empty()) {
    61	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(), destination.bytes(),
    62	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    63	    }
    64	}

    66	// Each logical vector cooperatively processes one CSR row.  Template
    67	// specialization lets short rows share a warp without wasting lanes, while
    68	// long rows use a full warp for coalesced value/index loads.  The generated
    69	// random matrix has tightly clustered row lengths, making this inexpensive
    70	// average-row-length dispatch especially effective.
    71	template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
    72	__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
    73	void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
    74	                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
    75	                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
    76	                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
    77	                   const index_t numRows,
    78	                   double* __restrict__ out) {
    79	    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&
    80	                  (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0);
    81	
    82	    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
    83	    const unsigned rowInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;
    84	    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
    85	    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x * ROWS_PER_BLOCK + rowInBlock);
    86	
    87	    double sum = 0.0;
    88	    if (row < numRows) {
    89	        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
    90	        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
    91	        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
    92	             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
    93	            sum = fma(val[element], vec[cols[element]], sum);
    94	        }
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    #pragma unroll
    98	    for (unsigned offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
    99	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {
   103	        out[row] = sum;
   104	    }
   105	}
   106	
   107	template <unsigned THREADS_PER_ROW>
   108	void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   109	                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   110	                      const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
   111	    constexpr unsigned ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
   112	    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(
   113	        (static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK);
   114	    spmvCsrKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
   115	        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);
   116	}

   118	unsigned selectThreadsPerRow(const index_t numRows, const index_t nItems) {
   119	    const double average = static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows;
   120	
   121	    // Start with the width that minimizes idle lanes for this row length.
   122	    unsigned width = 32;
   123	    if (average <= 1.0) {
   124	        width = 1;
   125	    } else if (average <= 2.0) {
   126	        width = 4;
   127	    } else if (average <= 8.0) {
   128	        width = 8;
   129	    } else if (average <= 32.0) {
   130	        width = 16;
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    // A width selected solely from row length can expose too few blocks for
   134	    // small matrices. Ensure roughly 1.5 blocks per SM so all processors get
   135	    // work, without forcing a full warp on every very short row.
   136	    int device = 0;
   137	    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
   138	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
   139	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
   140	    const uint64_t targetThreads = static_cast<uint64_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) *
   141	                                   CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE * 3 / 2;
   142	    while (width < 32 && static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * width < targetThreads) {
   143	        width *= 2;
   144	    }
   145	    return width;
   146	}
   147	
   148	void launchSpmv(const unsigned threadsPerRow,
   149	                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   150	                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   151	                const index_t numRows, double* out, cudaStream_t stream) {
   152	    switch (threadsPerRow) {
   153	        case 1:  launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
   154	        case 2:  launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
   155	        case 4:  launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
   156	        case 8:  launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
   157	        case 16: launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
   158	        default: launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream); break;
   159	    }
   160	}
   161	
   162	cudaGraphExec_t createSpmvGraph(const index_t launchCount,
   163	                                const unsigned threadsPerRow,
   164	                                const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   165	                                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   166	                                const index_t numRows, double* out,
   167	                                cudaStream_t stream) {
   168	    if (launchCount == 0) {
   169	        return nullptr;
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
   173	    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
   174	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
   175	    for (index_t launch = 0; launch < launchCount; ++launch) {
   176	        launchSpmv(threadsPerRow, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out, stream);
   177	    }
   178	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
   179	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   180	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
   181	    return executable;
   182	}
